RATIOS.mozjpeg;\n  const ratio = Math.max(0.005, Math.min(1.4, fn(quality)));\n  return Math.round(sourceBytes * ratio);\n}\n\n/** Formats bytes the way the reference readout does (\"862 kB\", \"2.79 MB\"). */\nexport function formatBytes(bytes: number): string {\n  const KB = 1024;\n  const MB = KB * 1024;\n  if (bytes >= MB) {\n    return `${(bytes / MB).toFixed(2)} MB`;\n  }\n  if (bytes >= KB) {\n    return `${Math.round(bytes / KB)} kB`;\n  }\n  return `${bytes} B`;\n}\n\n/** Percentage reduction relative to the source, 0-100. */\nexport function reductionPercent(sourceBytes: number, outputBytes: number): number {\n  if (sourceBytes <= 0) return 0;\n  const pct = (1 - outputBytes / sourceBytes) * 100;\n  return Math.max(0, Math.min(100, pct));\n}\n"}}]

Two stacked layers are clipped at the\n * divider; the handle moves with pointer drags.\n */\nexport function CompareView({\n  leftSrc,\n  rightSrc,\n  split,\n  onSplitChange,\n  leftAlt,\n  rightAlt,\n}: Props) {\n  const stageRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);\n  const dragging = useRef(false);\n\n  const updateFromClientX = useCallback(\n    (clientX: number) => {\n      const rect = stageRef.current?.getBoundingClientRect();\n      if (!rect || rect.width === 0) return;\n      const ratio = (clientX - rect.left) / rect.width;\n      onSplitChange(Math.max(0, Math.min(1, ratio)));\n    },\n    [onSplitChange],\n  );\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const onMove = (e: PointerEvent) => {\n      if (!dragging.current) return;\n      updateFromClientX(e.clientX);\n    };\n    const onUp = () => {\n      dragging.current = false;\n    };\n    window.addEventListener(\"pointermove\", onMove);\n    window.addEventListener(\"pointerup\", onUp);\n    return () => {\n      window.removeEventListener(\"pointermove\", onMove);\n      window.removeEventListener(\"pointerup\", onUp);\n    };\n  }, [updateFromClientX]);\n\n  const pct = `${split * 100}%`;\n\n  return (\n    <div\n      className=\"two-up\"\n      ref={stageRef}\n      style={{ [\"--split-point\" as string]: pct }}\n    >\n      <img\n        src={leftSrc}\n        alt={leftAlt}\n        className=\"clip-left\"\n        draggable={false}\n      />\n      <img\n        src={rightSrc}\n        alt={rightAlt}\n        className=\"clip-right\"\n        draggable={false}\n      />\n      <div\n        className=\"two-up-handle\"\n        role=\"slider\"\n        aria-label=\"Comparison position\"\n        aria-valuemin={0}\n        aria-valuemax={100}\n        aria-valuenow={Math.round(split * 100)}\n        tabIndex={0}\n        onPointerDown={(e) => {\n          dragging.current = true;\n          (e.target as HTMLElement).setPointerCapture?.(e.pointerId);\n        }}\n        onKeyDown={(e) => {\n          if (e.key === \"ArrowLeft\") onSplitChange(Math.max(0, split -
